Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also

    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;

    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
```

```
public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}
```

```
    //@ requires arr.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\result == -1) <==> (\forall int i,j; 0 <= i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);

            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}
```
Explanation of strengthening:
- Added to the postcondition for the case when a duplicate is found (\result >= 0):
  - The returned index \result is the minimal index with a duplicate later in the array.
  - For all indices k less than \result, no duplicates exist for arr[k] in the suffix (arr[k] != arr[l] for all l > k).
- This ensures the method returns the first element index with a duplicate, matching the original code's search order.
- Frame condition remains "assigns \nothing" since the method does not modify the input array.
- Behaviors remain disjoint and complete, covering null/empty array and non-empty array cases.

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))

    }
```

assistant: ```

    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
```

```
public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    //@           (\exists int j; \result < j < arr.length; arr[\result] == arr[j]) &&
    //@           (\forall int k; 0 <= k < \result; 
    //@               \forall int l; k < l < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[l]) &&
```
```
    //@           (\forall int m; 0 <= m < \result; 
    //@               \forall int n; m < n < arr.length; arr[m] != arr[n]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null || arr.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; 
        //@                 \forall int y; x < y < arr.length; arr[x] != arr[y]);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - 1 - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; i + 1 <= y < j; arr[i] != arr[y]);
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;

                    return i;
```

```java
public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ requires arr.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\result == -1) <==> (\forall int i,j; 0 <= i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length ==>
    //@           // There exists a duplicate of arr[result] at some index > result
    //@           (\exists int j; \result < j < arr.length; arr[\result] == arr[j]) &&
    //@           // No earlier index has any duplicates
    //@           (\forall int k; 0 <= k < \result; 
    //@               \forall int l; k < l < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[l]) &&
    //@           // The returned index is minimal with a duplicate
    //@           (\forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; 
    //@               \forall int j; i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]) &&
    //@           // Result is a valid index within array bounds
    //@           0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null || arr.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;

    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; 
        //@                 \forall int y; x < y < arr.length; arr[x] != arr[y]);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - 1 - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; i + 1 <= y < j; arr[i] != arr[y]);
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
```

```
public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
```

```
public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}
```

```
    //@           (\forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; 
    //@               \forall int j; i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]) &&
```
```
    //@           (\forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; 
    //@               \forall int j; i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null || arr.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; 
        //@                 \forall int y; x < y < arr.length; arr[x] != arr[y]);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - 1 - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; i + 1 <= y < j; arr[i] != arr[y]);
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
```

```
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;
```

```
    //@               \forall int j; i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]) &&
```
```
    //@           (\forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; 
    //@               \forall int j; i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null || arr.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; 
        //@                 \forall int y; x < y < arr.length; arr[x] != arr[y]);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - 1 - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; i + 1 <= y < j; arr[i] != arr[y]);
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}
```

```
public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ requires arr.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\result == -1) <==> (\forall int i,j; 0 <= i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length ==>
```
```
    //@           // There exists a duplicate of arr[result] at some index > result
    //@           (\exists int j; \result < j < arr.length; arr[\result] == arr[j]) &&
    //@           // No earlier index has any duplicates
    //@           (\forall int k; 0 <= k < \result; 
    //@               \forall int l; k < l < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[l]) &&
    //@           // The returned index is minimal with a duplicate
    //@           (\forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; 
    //@               \forall int j; i < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]) &&
    //@           // Result is a valid index within array bounds
    //@           0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null || arr.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;

    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; 
        //@                 \forall int y; x < y < arr.length; arr[x] != arr[y]);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - 1 - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; i + 1 <= y < j; arr[i] != arr[y]);
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}
```
